\displaystyle=\left\lvert\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{2n(2n-1)}{4n^{2}}\frac{(-1)^{(n-1)}(2(n-1))!}{4^{(n-1)}((n-1)!)^{2}}\varepsilon^{2(n-1)}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2(n-1)}\right\rvert_{\delta}.

Therefore, by the algebra property (
2.1
) and the bound
\left\lvert\frac{2n(2n-1)}{4n^{2}}\right\rvert\leq 1
, we get

\displaystyle\left\lvert\text{\rm Id}_{3\times 3}\left(\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}-1\right)\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(2n)!}{4^{n}(n!)^{2}}\varepsilon^{2n}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2n}=\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2}}{\left(1-\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2},

(A.4)

where in the last inequality we used the assumption in (
2.4
).
For the second term in (
A.2
), we use again the algebra property (
2.1
):

\displaystyle\left\lvert\varepsilon^{2}\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rvert_{\delta}\left\lvert\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)}\right\rvert_{\delta}=\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert v(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})\right\rvert_{\delta}\left\lvert\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)}\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}\left\lvert\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)}\right\rvert_{\delta},

(A.5)

where we used (
A.1
) to bound
\left\lvert v(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})\right\rvert_{\delta}
.
Then, for the last term, we have by the Taylor expansion and the algebra property (
2.1
)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)}\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle=\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\varepsilon^{2n}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2n}\right\rvert_{\delta}\leq\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\varepsilon^{2n}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2n}_{\delta}\leq\frac{\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}}{1-\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}_{\delta}}\leq 2\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta},

(A.6)

by assumption (
2.4
).
Therefore, going back to (
A.5
) using (
A.6
), we get

\displaystyle\left\lvert\varepsilon^{2}\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2}.

(A.7)

Finally, with (
A.4
) and (
A.7
) we bound (
A.2
):

\displaystyle\left\lvert\lambda(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lvert\text{\rm Id}_{3\times 3}\left(\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}-1\right)\right\rvert_{\delta}+\left\lvert\varepsilon^{2}\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right\rvert_{\delta}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lVert\xi\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}^{2}.

Then, for
\ell\in\{1,2,3\}
, we compute
\partial_{x_{\ell}}\lambda(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})
using (
A.2
):

\displaystyle\partial_{x_{\ell}}\left[\lambda(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})\right]=-\text{\rm Id}_{3\times 3}\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\cdot\partial_{x_{\ell}}\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)-\varepsilon^{2}\left(\frac{2\partial_{x_{\ell}}\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+\varepsilon^{4}(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})\left(\frac{3\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\cdot\partial_{x_{\ell}}\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{5}{2}}}\right).

Doing the same type of estimates as before on the three terms, we get

\displaystyle\left(\delta_{0}-\delta-\frac{t}{\eta}\right)^{\beta}\left\lvert\partial_{x_{\ell}}\lambda(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}\left(\delta_{0}-\delta-\frac{t}{\eta}\right)^{\beta}\left\lvert\partial_{x_{\ell}}\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}+C\varepsilon^{4}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{3}\left(\delta_{0}-\delta-\frac{t}{\eta}\right)^{\beta}\left\lvert\partial_{x_{\ell}}\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}^{4}.

Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert\lambda(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}^{4}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}^{2},

for
\varepsilon
small.
Finally, we prove (
2.8
). We can rewrite
v(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta})-v(\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta})
as follow

\displaystyle v\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right)-v\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right)=\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}}-\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt=\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}+\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt=(\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta})

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\times\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}+\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right)}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}\left(\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}+\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}\right)}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}}\right).

Then, using Taylor expansion as before, we can bound all denominators as follows

\displaystyle\left\lvert\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}}\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(2n)!}{4^{n}(n!)^{2}}\varepsilon^{2n}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2n}=\frac{1}{\left(1-\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq C,

where we used assumption (
2.4
) on
\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}
and
\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}
. Hence

\displaystyle\left\lvert v(\xi^{(1)})-v(\xi^{(2)})\right\rvert_{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}\left(\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}+\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert_{\delta}\right)+1\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}},

where we used assumption (
2.7
) for the last inequality.
The estimates for
\partial_{x_{\ell}}\left(v(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta})-v(\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta})\right)
for
\ell\in\{1,2,3\}
are done similarly.
Thus, we get our result:

\displaystyle\left\lVert v(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta})-v(\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta})\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rVert_{\delta_{0}}.

Concerning inequality (
2.9
), we have

\displaystyle\lambda\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right)-\lambda\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right)=\text{Id}_{3\times 3}\Bigg(\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}-\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{2}\Bigg(\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right)\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{-\frac{3}{2}}-\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right)\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Bigg).

Since

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right)\cdot\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}+\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right)}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}\left(\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}+\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}}\right)},

and

\displaystyle\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}=\frac{\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right)\otimes\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}+\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\otimes\left(\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}-\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right)}{\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\xi_{\Theta}^{\varepsilon,(2)}\otimes\xi_{\Theta}^{\varepsilon,(2)}\Bigg(\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{-\frac{3}{2}}-\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\right)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Bigg),

we can proceed as in (
2.8
) to get the result.
∎

Proof of Lemma
2.3
.

By recalling the Taylor expression (
A.3
), we can write

\displaystyle v(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})-\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{2}|\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}|^{2}}}-\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}=\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n}(2n)!}{4^{n}(n!)^{2}}\varepsilon^{2n}|\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}|^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{2n(2n-1)}{4n^{2}}\frac{(-1)^{(n-1)}(2(n-1))!}{4^{(n-1)}((n-1)!)^{2}}\varepsilon^{2(n-1)}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rvert^{2(n-1)}.

Therefore, by using that
\left\lvert\frac{2n(2n-1)}{4n^{2}}\right\rvert\leq 1
and the algebra property for the Sobolev space
H^{s}_{x}
with
s>\frac{3}{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert v(\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta})-\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rVert_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t}\left\lVert\varepsilon^{2}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}(t)\right\rvert^{3}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(2n)!}{4^{n}(n!)^{2}}\varepsilon^{2n}\left\lvert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}(t)\right\rvert^{2n}\right\rVert_{H^{s}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}(t)\right\rVert^{3}_{H^{s}_{x}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(2n)!}{4^{n}(n!)^{2}}\varepsilon^{2n}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}(t)\right\rVert^{2n}_{H^{s}_{x}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t}\Bigg(\varepsilon^{2}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}(t)\right\rVert^{3}_{H^{s}_{x}}\frac{1}{\big(1-\varepsilon^{2}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}(t)\right\rVert^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Bigg)\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left\lVert\xi^{\varepsilon}_{\Theta}\right\rVert^{3}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}_{x}},

where we used the Taylor expansion (
A.3
) for the penultimate inequality and assumption (
2.10
) for the last one.

∎
